整体结构是对的，但有 3 个地方会编译报错，必须修：
❌ 问题 1：RabbitMqConfig 导包错误
你导的是 java.util.Queue，但 new Queue() 用的是 Spring AMQP 的 Queue。
修正：
java
package com.shelf.donate.config;

import org.springframework.amqp.core.Binding;
import org.springframework.amqp.core.BindingBuilder;
import org.springframework.amqp.core.DirectExchange;
import org.springframework.amqp.core.Queue;  // ← 改这里，不是 java.util.Queue
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

@Configuration
public class RabbitMqConfig {
    // ... 下面不变
}
❌ 问题 2：CourseBook 缺字段，DonateServiceImpl 会 NPE
你的 CourseBook 实体只有 id/grade/major/courseName/isbn/isRequired，但 DonateServiceImpl 里调了 cb.getAuthor() 和 cb.getPublisher()，编译直接报错。
修正 CourseBook 实体：
java
package com.shelf.donate.entity;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.IdType;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.TableId;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.TableName;
import lombok.Data;

@Data
@TableName("course_book")
public class CourseBook {

    @TableId(type = IdType.AUTO)
    private Long id;

    private Integer grade;

    private String major;

    private String courseName;

    private String isbn;

    private String author;        // ← 加上

    private String publisher;     // ← 加上

    private Integer isRequired;
}
对应的建表 SQL 也要加这两个字段：
sql
ALTER TABLE course_book ADD COLUMN author VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT '' COMMENT '作者';
ALTER TABLE course_book ADD COLUMN publisher VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL DEFAULT '' COMMENT '出版社';
❌ 问题 3：CourseBookServiceImpl 缺 listBooks 实现
你的 Mapper 里有 selectBooks，但 CourseBookServiceImpl 里没实现 listBooks 方法。如果 ICourseBookService 接口里有这个方法（之前我给的接口里有），这里会编译报错。
补上：
java
@Service
public class CourseBookServiceImpl extends ServiceImpl<CourseBookMapper, CourseBook> implements ICourseBookService {

    @Override
    public List<String> listMajors(Integer grade) {
        return baseMapper.selectMajors(grade);
    }

    @Override
    public List<String> listCourses(Integer grade, String major) {
        return baseMapper.selectCourses(grade, major);
    }

    // ← 补上这个
    @Override
    public List<CourseBook> listBooks(Integer grade, String major, String courseName) {
        return baseMapper.selectBooks(grade, major, courseName);
    }
}

好的，申领（shelf-borrow）是核心链路，涉及 Feign 调 user 查状态 → Feign 调 donate 扣库存 → 生成本地记录 → 发 MQ 延迟消息。我一次性给全。
一、shelf-api 模块新增（Feign 契约）
borrow 要调 donate 扣库存，必须在 shelf-api 里定义好接口和 DTO。
1. DonateFeignApi.java
java
package com.shelf.api.donate;

import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.*;
import com.shelf.common.result.Result;
import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.FeignClient;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;

@FeignClient(name = "shelf-donate", path = "/api/donate")
public interface DonateFeignApi {

    @PostMapping("/stock/deduct")
    Result<BookInstanceDTO> deductStock(@RequestBody DeductStockRequest request);

    @PostMapping("/stock/release")
    Result<Void> releaseStock(@RequestBody ReleaseStockRequest request);

    @PostMapping("/stock/return")
    Result<Void> returnStock(@RequestBody ReturnStockRequest request);
}

package com.shelf.borrow.entity;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.IdType;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.TableId;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.annotation.TableName;
import lombok.Data;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Data
@TableName("borrow_record")
public class BorrowRecord {

    @TableId(type = IdType.AUTO)
    private Long id;

    private String recordNo;       // 凭证号 BR202608190001

    private String requestId;      // 幂等键

    private Long userId;

    private Long instanceId;       // 分配的实体书ID

    private String isbn;

    private String bookTitle;      // 书名冗余

    private String status;         // PENDING_PICKUP / BORROWED / RETURNED / OVERDUE / CANCELLED

    private LocalDateTime borrowTime;

    private LocalDateTime pickupDeadline;  // 领取截止（7天后）

    private LocalDateTime pickupTime;      // 实际领取时间

    private LocalDateTime dueDate;         // 到期时间（领取后一学期）

    private LocalDateTime returnTime;

    private String qrCode;

    private LocalDateTime createTime;

    private LocalDateTime updateTime;
}

6. MQ 配置 + 消息体
java
// BorrowRabbitMqConfig.java
package com.shelf.borrow.config;

import org.springframework.amqp.core.Binding;
import org.springframework.amqp.core.BindingBuilder;
import org.springframework.amqp.core.CustomExchange;
import org.springframework.amqp.core.Queue;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

@Configuration
public class BorrowRabbitMqConfig {

    public static final String BORROW_EXCHANGE = "shelf.borrow.exchange";
    public static final String PICKUP_TIMEOUT_QUEUE = "borrow.pickup.timeout.queue";
    public static final String PICKUP_TIMEOUT_KEY = "borrow.pickup.timeout";

    @Bean
    public CustomExchange borrowExchange() {
        Map<String, Object> args = new HashMap<>();
        args.put("x-delayed-type", "direct");
        return new CustomExchange(BORROW_EXCHANGE, "x-delayed-message", true, false, args);
    }

    @Bean
    public Queue pickupTimeoutQueue() {
        return new Queue(PICKUP_TIMEOUT_QUEUE, true);
    }

    @Bean
    public Binding pickupTimeoutBinding() {
        return BindingBuilder.bind(pickupTimeoutQueue())
                .to(borrowExchange())
                .with(PICKUP_TIMEOUT_KEY)
                .noargs();
    }
}

7. Service（核心）
java
package com.shelf.borrow.service;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.service.IService;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.dto.ApplyDTO;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.vo.BorrowRecordVO;
import com.shelf.borrow.entity.BorrowRecord;

java
package com.shelf.borrow.service.impl;

import cn.hutool.core.bean.BeanUtil;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.update.UpdateWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.service.impl.ServiceImpl;
import com.shelf.api.donate.DonateFeignApi;
import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.BookInstanceDTO;
import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.DeductStockRequest;
import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.ReleaseStockRequest;
import com.shelf.api.user.UserFeignApi;
import com.shelf.api.user.dto.UserStatusDTO;
import com.shelf.borrow.config.BorrowRabbitMqConfig;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.dto.ApplyDTO;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.vo.BorrowRecordVO;
import com.shelf.borrow.entity.BorrowRecord;
import com.shelf.borrow.mapper.BorrowRecordMapper;
import com.shelf.borrow.mq.dto.PickupTimeoutMessage;
import com.shelf.borrow.service.IBorrowService;
import com.shelf.common.context.UserContext;
import com.shelf.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.shelf.common.result.ErrorCode;
import com.shelf.common.result.Result;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.amqp.rabbit.core.RabbitTemplate;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class BorrowServiceImpl extends ServiceImpl<BorrowRecordMapper, BorrowRecord> implements IBorrowService {

    private final UserFeignApi userFeignApi;
    private final DonateFeignApi donateFeignApi;
    private final RabbitTemplate rabbitTemplate;
    private final StringRedisTemplate redisTemplate;

    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public BorrowRecordVO apply(ApplyDTO dto) {
        Long userId = UserContext.getUserId();
        if (userId == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.UNAUTHORIZED, "用户未登录");
        }

        // 1. Feign 调 user 查状态
        Result<UserStatusDTO> userResult = userFeignApi.getUserStatus(userId);
        if (!userResult.isSuccess() || userResult.getData() == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.SYSTEM_ERROR, "用户服务调用失败");
        }
        UserStatusDTO userStatus = userResult.getData();
        if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(userStatus.getFrozen())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, "账号已冻结，无法申领");
        }
        if (userStatus.getCurrentBorrowCount() != null && userStatus.getCurrentBorrowCount() >= 5) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, "借阅数量已达上限（5本）");
        }

        // 2. 检查是否已借过同一 ISBN（未归还）
        Long existCount = baseMapper.selectCount(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                        .eq(BorrowRecord::getUserId, userId)
                        .eq(BorrowRecord::getIsbn, dto.getIsbn())
                        .in(BorrowRecord::getStatus, Arrays.asList("PENDING_PICKUP", "BORROWED", "OVERDUE"))
        );
        if (existCount != null && existCount > 0) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "您已借阅该教材，归还后可再次申领");
        }

        // 3. Feign 调 donate 扣库存
        DeductStockRequest deductReq = new DeductStockRequest();
        deductReq.setIsbn(dto.getIsbn());
        Result<BookInstanceDTO> deductResult = donateFeignApi.deductStock(deductReq);
        if (!deductResult.isSuccess() || deductResult.getData() == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "库存不足或扣减失败");
        }
        BookInstanceDTO instance = deductResult.getData();

        try {
            // 4. 生成本地借阅记录
            BorrowRecord record = new BorrowRecord();
            record.setRecordNo(generateRecordNo());
            record.setUserId(userId);
            record.setInstanceId(instance.getInstanceId());
            record.setIsbn(dto.getIsbn());
            record.setBookTitle(instance.getTitle());
            record.setStatus("PENDING_PICKUP");
            record.setBorrowTime(LocalDateTime.now());
            record.setPickupDeadline(LocalDateTime.now().plusDays(7));
            record.setQrCode(generateQrCode(record.getRecordNo()));
            baseMapper.insert(record);

            // 5. 发 MQ 延迟消息（7天后超时释放）
            PickupTimeoutMessage timeoutMsg = new PickupTimeoutMessage();
            timeoutMsg.setRecordNo(record.getRecordNo());
            timeoutMsg.setInstanceId(instance.getInstanceId());
            timeoutMsg.setIsbn(dto.getIsbn());
            timeoutMsg.setUserId(userId);

            rabbitTemplate.convertAndSend(
                    BorrowRabbitMqConfig.BORROW_EXCHANGE,
                    BorrowRabbitMqConfig.PICKUP_TIMEOUT_KEY,
                    timeoutMsg,
                    message -> {
                        message.getMessageProperties().setDelay(7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
                        return message;
                    }
            );

            // 6. 组装返回
            BorrowRecordVO vo = new BorrowRecordVO();
            BeanUtil.copyProperties(record, vo);
            vo.setInstanceCode(instance.getInstanceCode());
            vo.setLocation(instance.getLocation());
            return vo;

        } catch (Exception e) {
            // 补偿：释放库存
            log.error("申领失败，补偿释放库存: instanceId={}, isbn={}", instance.getInstanceId(), dto.getIsbn());
            ReleaseStockRequest releaseReq = new ReleaseStockRequest();
            releaseReq.setInstanceId(instance.getInstanceId());
            releaseReq.setIsbn(dto.getIsbn());
            try {
                donateFeignApi.releaseStock(releaseReq);
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                log.error("补偿释放库存失败: {}", ex.getMessage());
            }
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.SYSTEM_ERROR, "申领失败，请重试");
        }
    }

    @Override
    public List<BorrowRecordVO> listMyBorrows() {
        Long userId = UserContext.getUserId();
        List<BorrowRecord> list = baseMapper.selectList(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                        .eq(BorrowRecord::getUserId, userId)
                        .orderByDesc(BorrowRecord::getCreateTime));
        return list.stream().map(r -> {
            BorrowRecordVO vo = new BorrowRecordVO();
            BeanUtil.copyProperties(r, vo);
            return vo;
        }).collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    private String generateRecordNo() {
        String day = LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMdd"));
        String key = "shelf:borrow:seq:" + day;
        Long seq = redisTemplate.opsForValue().increment(key);
        if (seq == null) seq = 1L;
        return String.format("BR%s%06d", day, seq);
    }

    private String generateQrCode(String recordNo) {
        return java.util.Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(recordNo.getBytes());
    }
}
8. MQ 消费者（7 天超时释放）
java
package com.shelf.borrow.mq.consumer;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.update.UpdateWrapper;
import com.rabbitmq.client.Channel;
import com.shelf.api.donate.DonateFeignApi;
import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.ReleaseStockRequest;
import com.shelf.borrow.entity.BorrowRecord;
import com.shelf.borrow.mapper.BorrowRecordMapper;
import com.shelf.borrow.mq.dto.PickupTimeoutMessage;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.amqp.core.Message;
import org.springframework.amqp.rabbit.annotation.RabbitListener;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.io.IOException;

@Slf4j
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class PickupTimeoutConsumer {

    private final BorrowRecordMapper borrowRecordMapper;
    private final DonateFeignApi donateFeignApi;

    @RabbitListener(queues = "borrow.pickup.timeout.queue")
    public void handle(PickupTimeoutMessage msg, Channel channel, Message message) throws IOException {
        long deliveryTag = message.getMessageProperties().getDeliveryTag();

        try {
            log.info("收到超时释放消息: recordNo={}", msg.getRecordNo());

            // 幂等：查记录状态
            BorrowRecord record = borrowRecordMapper.selectOne(
                    new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                            .eq(BorrowRecord::getRecordNo, msg.getRecordNo()));
            if (record == null || !"PENDING_PICKUP".equals(record.getStatus())) {
                log.warn("记录已处理或不存在，跳过: recordNo={}", msg.getRecordNo());
                channel.basicAck(deliveryTag, false);
                return;
            }

            // 1. 更新记录为已取消
            borrowRecordMapper.update(null,
                    new UpdateWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                            .eq("id", record.getId())
                            .eq("status", "PENDING_PICKUP")
                            .set("status", "CANCELLED"));

            // 2. 释放库存（Feign 调 donate）
            ReleaseStockRequest releaseReq = new ReleaseStockRequest();
            releaseReq.setInstanceId(msg.getInstanceId());
            releaseReq.setIsbn(msg.getIsbn());
            donateFeignApi.releaseStock(releaseReq);

            log.info("超时释放完成: recordNo={}", msg.getRecordNo());
            channel.basicAck(deliveryTag, false);

        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("超时释放失败: recordNo={}, error={}", msg.getRecordNo(), e.getMessage());
            channel.basicNack(deliveryTag, false, true);
        }
    }
}

package com.shelf.borrow.controller;

四、关键提醒
表格
事项	说明
RabbitMQ 延迟插件	x-delayed-message 需要提前安装插件：rabbitmq-plugins enable rabbitmq_delayed_message_exchange，否则启动报错
Feign 调用失败	如果 donate 的 deductStock 没实现，borrow 申领会报 "库存不足或扣减失败"
二维码	MVP 用 Base64 编码 recordNo，扫码页面解析后查 borrow_record
7 天延迟	setDelay(604800000) 毫秒，消息 7 天后自动进入 PickupTimeoutConsumer
申领链路已经完整了，下一步你可以：
先补 donate 的 deductStock 实现（Redis + 乐观锁分配实体书）
或者先写归还接口（扫码归还，调 donate returnStock + 更新 borrow_record）
也就是你目前实现的代码还是简单的申领的代码是吧，还没有引入这个lua脚本，或者redission这个分布式锁，或者这个幂等性这些技术是吧，还有这个mq这里延迟消息时间这么长使用mq是不是不太合适呀，还有我这个项目测试阶段什么的，或者以后也不可能二十四小时都在运行的，这个电脑重启后这个mq里面的申领这些信息可能会丢啊，我记得之前探讨的时候是那个什么存到数据库里面了，把这个时间什么的存到数据库里面了，然后有个定时任务spring task，虽然这个微服务使用这个task不太好吧，但是目前比较简单的一个方案就是使用task这个定时任务和简单的redis锁之类的来进行这个期限这个定时任务呀，你再想一下，怎么做才能合理一点，
编辑
复制
分享
微服务轻量化 用定时任务替代 MQ 延迟队列
你说得非常对，当前代码是简化版，而且MQ 延迟 7 天对个人项目/测试环境确实不友好。
核心问题：
你电脑不可能 24 小时开着，RabbitMQ 重启后如果消息没持久化或者消费者没确认，7 天的延迟消息直接消失
测试阶段频繁重启服务，延迟队列里的消息全丢，书永远卡在 RESERVED 状态
之前另一个 AI 提过数据库记录时间 + 定时任务扫描，那个方案对个人项目更稳
修正后的务实方案
保留 MQ 的地方：只有捐赠异步入库（即时消费，秒级完成，重启后最多重发一次，无影响）
去掉 MQ 的地方：
❌ 申领 7 天超时释放
❌ 到期前 3 天提醒
❌ 逾期扫描
替换为：数据库记录截止时间 + Spring @Scheduled 定时任务 + Redis 分布式锁
表格
场景	原方案	新方案
7 天未领取释放	MQ 延迟 7 天	定时任务每 30 分钟扫描 pickup_deadline < NOW()
到期提醒	MQ 动态延迟	定时任务每天早 9 点扫描 due_date 在 3 天内且未提醒的
逾期标记	MQ 延迟	定时任务每天扫描 due_date < NOW() 且状态为 BORROWED 的
为什么这个方案更合理？
消息不丢：时间存在数据库里，重启 100 次任务照样扫得到
测试友好：你随时能手动改 pickup_deadline 为过去时间，立刻触发超时释放，不用等 7 天
简单可维护：@Scheduled 是 Spring 原生，不需要 MQ 插件、不需要考虑 TTL 阻塞
锁竞争可控：用 Redis SETNX 做个简单分布式锁，防止多实例同时跑任务
需要改什么？
1. 去掉 Borrow 服务的 MQ 延迟相关代码
删除：
BorrowRabbitMqConfig 里的 x-delayed-message 交换机
PickupTimeoutMessage DTO
PickupTimeoutConsumer 消费者
BorrowServiceImpl.apply() 里发 MQ 的代码
保留（donate 服务的）：
DonateInboundConsumer（捐赠异步入库，即时消费，没问题）
2. BorrowRecord 表不变，但逻辑更直观
sql
-- 已有的字段完全够用
pickup_deadline  DATETIME  NOT NULL  -- 申领后7天
due_date         DATETIME  NULL      -- 领取后一学期
remind_time      DATETIME  NULL      -- 提醒发送时间（NULL=未提醒）
3. 新增定时任务类（核心）
java
package com.shelf.borrow.task;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.update.UpdateWrapper;
import com.shelf.api.donate.DonateFeignApi;
import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.ReleaseStockRequest;
import com.shelf.borrow.entity.BorrowRecord;
import com.shelf.borrow.mapper.BorrowRecordMapper;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

@Slf4j
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class BorrowScheduledTask {

    private final BorrowRecordMapper borrowRecordMapper;
    private final DonateFeignApi donateFeignApi;
    private final StringRedisTemplate redisTemplate;

    /**
     * 分布式锁 Key 前缀
     */
    private static final String LOCK_PREFIX = "shelf:task:lock:";

    /**
     * 1. 超时释放：每 30 分钟扫描一次
     * 把 PENDING_PICKUP 且 pickup_deadline 已过的记录取消掉
     */
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 0/30 * * * ?")
    public void releaseTimeoutRecords() {
        String lockKey = LOCK_PREFIX + "releaseTimeout";
        if (!tryLock(lockKey, 600)) {  // 锁10分钟
            log.info("超时释放任务已被其他实例执行，跳过");
            return;
        }

        try {
            log.info("开始扫描超时未领取记录");
            LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();

            List<BorrowRecord> timeoutList = borrowRecordMapper.selectList(
                    new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                            .eq(BorrowRecord::getStatus, "PENDING_PICKUP")
                            .lt(BorrowRecord::getPickupDeadline, now)
                            .last("LIMIT 100")  // 每次最多处理100条
            );

            for (BorrowRecord record : timeoutList) {
                try {
                    // 1. 更新记录为已取消
                    borrowRecordMapper.update(null,
                            new UpdateWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                                    .eq("id", record.getId())
                                    .eq("status", "PENDING_PICKUP")  // 乐观锁
                                    .set("status", "CANCELLED"));

                    // 2. 释放库存（调 donate）
                    ReleaseStockRequest req = new ReleaseStockRequest();
                    req.setInstanceId(record.getInstanceId());
                    req.setIsbn(record.getIsbn());
                    donateFeignApi.releaseStock(req);

                    log.info("超时释放成功: recordNo={}", record.getRecordNo());

                } catch (Exception e) {
                    log.error("超时释放失败: recordNo={}, error={}", record.getRecordNo(), e.getMessage());
                    // 失败就失败，下次定时任务还会扫到（如果状态还是PENDING_PICKUP）
                    // 或者进死信表，MVP先打日志
                }
            }
        } finally {
            releaseLock(lockKey);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 2. 到期提醒：每天早 9 点扫描
     * 扫 BORROWED 状态，due_date 在未来3天内，且 remind_time 为 NULL 的
     */
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 0 9 * * ?")
    public void remindDueRecords() {
        String lockKey = LOCK_PREFIX + "remindDue";
        if (!tryLock(lockKey, 3600)) return;  // 锁1小时

        try {
            log.info("开始扫描到期提醒");
            LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
            LocalDateTime threeDaysLater = now.plusDays(3);

            List<BorrowRecord> remindList = borrowRecordMapper.selectList(
                    new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                            .eq(BorrowRecord::getStatus, "BORROWED")
                            .isNull(BorrowRecord::getRemindTime)
                            .lt(BorrowRecord::getDueDate, threeDaysLater)
                            .ge(BorrowRecord::getDueDate, now)  // 还没到期
                            .last("LIMIT 100")
            );

            for (BorrowRecord record : remindList) {
                try {
                    // TODO: 调用通知服务（站内信/短信/微信），MVP 先打日志
                    log.info("到期提醒: userId={}, recordNo={}, dueDate={}",
                            record.getUserId(), record.getRecordNo(), record.getDueDate());

                    // 标记已提醒
                    borrowRecordMapper.update(null,
                            new UpdateWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                                    .eq("id", record.getId())
                                    .set("remind_time", now));

                } catch (Exception e) {
                    log.error("提醒失败: recordNo={}", record.getRecordNo());
                }
            }
        } finally {
            releaseLock(lockKey);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 3. 逾期扫描：每天早 9 点 05 分（提醒之后）
     * 把 BORROWED 且 due_date < NOW() 的改为 OVERDUE
     */
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 5 9 * * ?")
    public void markOverdueRecords() {
        String lockKey = LOCK_PREFIX + "markOverdue";
        if (!tryLock(lockKey, 3600)) return;

        try {
            log.info("开始扫描逾期记录");
            LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();

            List<BorrowRecord> overdueList = borrowRecordMapper.selectList(
                    new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                            .eq(BorrowRecord::getStatus, "BORROWED")
                            .lt(BorrowRecord::getDueDate, now)
                            .last("LIMIT 100")
            );

            for (BorrowRecord record : overdueList) {
                try {
                    borrowRecordMapper.update(null,
                            new UpdateWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                                    .eq("id", record.getId())
                                    .eq("status", "BORROWED")
                                    .set("status", "OVERDUE"));

                    // TODO: 调 user 服务加逾期次数
                    log.info("标记逾期: recordNo={}", record.getRecordNo());

                } catch (Exception e) {
                    log.error("标记逾期失败: recordNo={}", record.getRecordNo());
                }
            }
        } finally {
            releaseLock(lockKey);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 简单 Redis 分布式锁（SETNX）
     */
    private boolean tryLock(String key, long seconds) {
        Boolean success = redisTemplate.opsForValue().setIfAbsent(key, "1", seconds, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        return Boolean.TRUE.equals(success);
    }

    private void releaseLock(String key) {
        redisTemplate.delete(key);
    }
}

@Override
@Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
public BorrowRecordVO apply(ApplyDTO dto) {
    // ... 前面的校验不变 ...

    // Feign 调 donate 扣库存
    Result<BookInstanceDTO> deductResult = donateFeignApi.deductStock(deductReq);
    if (!deductResult.isSuccess() || deductResult.getData() == null) {
        throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "库存不足");
    }
    BookInstanceDTO instance = deductResult.getData();

    try {

        BorrowRecord record = new BorrowRecord();

        record.setUserId(userId);
        record.setInstanceId(instance.getInstanceId());
        record.setIsbn(dto.getIsbn());
        record.setBookTitle(instance.getTitle());
        record.setStatus("PENDING_PICKUP");
        record.setBorrowTime(LocalDateTime.now());
        record.setPickupDeadline(LocalDateTime.now().plusDays(7));  // ← 7天截止时间写死在这里
        record.setQrCode(generateQrCode(record.getRecordNo()));
        baseMapper.insert(record);

        // 去掉发 MQ 的代码！

        // 组装返回...
        return vo;

    } catch (Exception e) {
        // 补偿释放库存（不变）
        ...
        throw e;
    }
}
最终 MQ 使用清单
表格
服务	队列	用途	保留/删除
shelf-donate	donate.inbound.queue	捐赠异步入库（即时消费）	✅ 保留
shelf-borrow	borrow.pickup.timeout.queue	7 天超时释放	❌ 删除，改定时任务
shelf-borrow	borrow.due.remind.queue	到期提醒	❌ 删除，改定时任务
这样改的好处
测试随意重启：时间存在数据库，重启后定时任务继续扫，不丢任务
调试方便：把 pickup_deadline 改成 2026-08-19 17:20:00，等 30 分钟定时任务一到立刻触发超时，不用等 7 天
不依赖 MQ 插件：不需要装 rabbitmq_delayed_message_exchange
分布式锁防重：多实例部署时只有一台跑任务，不会重复释放
面试能讲："MVP 阶段用定时任务 + Redis 锁保证最终一致性，后续数据量大了再切 XXL-Job"
下一步
把 borrow 服务里 MQ 延迟相关的代码删掉（Consumer、Config 里的延迟交换机、发消息代码）
加上上面的 BorrowScheduledTask
启动类加 @EnableScheduling
写 donate 服务的 deductStock 实现（Redis + 乐观锁分配实体书）
这样架构就清爽了：MQ 只做捐赠异步入库，时间驱动的任务全交给定时任务扫描。
那你能把最终的这个申领这边的再给我一下吗，我现在搞乱了，你再给我一下吧

八、ServiceImpl（核心）
java
package com.shelf.borrow.service.impl;

import cn.hutool.core.bean.BeanUtil;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.service.impl.ServiceImpl;
import com.shelf.api.donate.DonateFeignApi;
import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.BookInstanceDTO;
import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.DeductStockRequest;
import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.ReleaseStockRequest;
import com.shelf.api.user.UserFeignApi;
import com.shelf.api.user.dto.UserStatusDTO;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.dto.ApplyDTO;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.vo.BorrowRecordVO;
import com.shelf.borrow.entity.BorrowRecord;
import com.shelf.borrow.mapper.BorrowRecordMapper;
import com.shelf.borrow.service.IBorrowService;
import com.shelf.common.context.UserContext;
import com.shelf.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.shelf.common.result.ErrorCode;
import com.shelf.common.result.Result;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class BorrowServiceImpl extends ServiceImpl<BorrowRecordMapper, BorrowRecord> implements IBorrowService {

    private final UserFeignApi userFeignApi;
    private final DonateFeignApi donateFeignApi;
    private final StringRedisTemplate redisTemplate;

    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public BorrowRecordVO apply(ApplyDTO dto) {
        Long userId = UserContext.getUserId();
        if (userId == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.UNAUTHORIZED, "用户未登录");
        }

        // 1. Feign 调 user 查状态
        Result<UserStatusDTO> userResult = userFeignApi.getUserStatus(userId);
        if (!userResult.isSuccess() || userResult.getData() == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.SYSTEM_ERROR, "用户服务调用失败");
        }
        UserStatusDTO userStatus = userResult.getData();
        if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(userStatus.getFrozen())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, "账号已冻结，无法申领");
        }
        if (userStatus.getCurrentBorrowCount() != null && userStatus.getCurrentBorrowCount() >= 5) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, "借阅数量已达上限（5本）");
        }

        // 2. 检查是否已借过同一 ISBN（未归还）
        Long existCount = baseMapper.selectCount(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                        .eq(BorrowRecord::getUserId, userId)
                        .eq(BorrowRecord::getIsbn, dto.getIsbn())
                        .in(BorrowRecord::getStatus, Arrays.asList("PENDING_PICKUP", "BORROWED", "OVERDUE"))
        );
        if (existCount != null && existCount > 0) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "您已借阅该教材，归还后可再次申领");
        }

        // 3. Feign 调 donate 扣库存
        DeductStockRequest deductReq = new DeductStockRequest();
        deductReq.setIsbn(dto.getIsbn());
        Result<BookInstanceDTO> deductResult = donateFeignApi.deductStock(deductReq);
        if (!deductResult.isSuccess() || deductResult.getData() == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "库存不足或扣减失败");
        }
        BookInstanceDTO instance = deductResult.getData();

        try {
            // 4. 生成本地借阅记录
            BorrowRecord record = new BorrowRecord();
            record.setRecordNo(generateRecordNo());
            record.setUserId(userId);
            record.setInstanceId(instance.getInstanceId());
            record.setIsbn(dto.getIsbn());
            record.setBookTitle(instance.getTitle());
            record.setStatus("PENDING_PICKUP");
            record.setBorrowTime(LocalDateTime.now());
            record.setPickupDeadline(LocalDateTime.now().plusDays(7));
            record.setQrCode(generateQrCode(record.getRecordNo()));
            baseMapper.insert(record);

            // 5. 组装返回
            BorrowRecordVO vo = new BorrowRecordVO();
            BeanUtil.copyProperties(record, vo);
            vo.setInstanceCode(instance.getInstanceCode());
            vo.setLocation(instance.getLocation());
            return vo;

        } catch (Exception e) {
            // 补偿：释放库存
            log.error("申领失败，补偿释放库存: instanceId={}, isbn={}", instance.getInstanceId(), dto.getIsbn());
            ReleaseStockRequest releaseReq = new ReleaseStockRequest();
            releaseReq.setInstanceId(instance.getInstanceId());
            releaseReq.setIsbn(dto.getIsbn());
            try {
                donateFeignApi.releaseStock(releaseReq);
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                log.error("补偿释放库存失败: {}", ex.getMessage());
            }
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.SYSTEM_ERROR, "申领失败，请重试");
        }
    }

    @Override
    public List<BorrowRecordVO> listMyBorrows() {
        Long userId = UserContext.getUserId();
        List<BorrowRecord> list = baseMapper.selectList(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                        .eq(BorrowRecord::getUserId, userId)
                        .orderByDesc(BorrowRecord::getCreateTime));
        return list.stream().map(r -> {
            BorrowRecordVO vo = new BorrowRecordVO();
            BeanUtil.copyProperties(r, vo);
            return vo;
        }).collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    private String generateRecordNo() {
        String day = LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMdd"));
        String key = "shelf:borrow:seq:" + day;
        Long seq = redisTemplate.opsForValue().increment(key);
        if (seq == null) seq = 1L;
        return String.format("BR%s%06d", day, seq);
    }

    private String generateQrCode(String recordNo) {
        return java.util.Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(recordNo.getBytes());
    }
}
九、Controller
java
package com.shelf.borrow.controller;

import cn.dev33.satoken.annotation.SaCheckLogin;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.dto.ApplyDTO;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.vo.BorrowRecordVO;
import com.shelf.borrow.service.IBorrowService;
import com.shelf.common.result.Result;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.util.List;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/borrow")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class BorrowController {

    private final IBorrowService borrowService;

    @PostMapping("/apply")
    @SaCheckLogin
    public Result<BorrowRecordVO> apply(@Valid @RequestBody ApplyDTO dto) {
        return Result.success(borrowService.apply(dto));
    }

    @GetMapping("/my-records")
    @SaCheckLogin
    public Result<List<BorrowRecordVO>> listMyBorrows() {
        return Result.success(borrowService.listMyBorrows());
    }
}
十、定时任务（核心，替代 MQ）
java
package com.shelf.borrow.task;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.update.UpdateWrapper;
import com.shelf.api.donate.DonateFeignApi;
import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.ReleaseStockRequest;
import com.shelf.borrow.entity.BorrowRecord;
import com.shelf.borrow.mapper.BorrowRecordMapper;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

@Slf4j
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class BorrowScheduledTask {

    private final BorrowRecordMapper borrowRecordMapper;
    private final DonateFeignApi donateFeignApi;
    private final StringRedisTemplate redisTemplate;

    private static final String LOCK_PREFIX = "shelf:task:lock:";

    /**
     * 1. 超时释放：每 30 分钟扫描一次
     * PENDING_PICKUP 且 pickup_deadline 已过 → 取消 + 释放库存
     */
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 0/30 * * * ?")
    public void releaseTimeoutRecords() {
        String lockKey = LOCK_PREFIX + "releaseTimeout";
        if (!tryLock(lockKey, 600)) {
            log.info("超时释放任务已被其他实例执行，跳过");
            return;
        }

        try {
            log.info("开始扫描超时未领取记录");
            LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();

            List<BorrowRecord> timeoutList = borrowRecordMapper.selectList(
                    new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                            .eq(BorrowRecord::getStatus, "PENDING_PICKUP")
                            .lt(BorrowRecord::getPickupDeadline, now)
                            .last("LIMIT 100")
            );

            for (BorrowRecord record : timeoutList) {
                try {
                    // 乐观锁更新状态
                    borrowRecordMapper.update(null,
                            new UpdateWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                                    .eq("id", record.getId())
                                    .eq("status", "PENDING_PICKUP")
                                    .set("status", "CANCELLED"));

                    // 调 donate 释放库存
                    ReleaseStockRequest req = new ReleaseStockRequest();
                    req.setInstanceId(record.getInstanceId());
                    req.setIsbn(record.getIsbn());
                    donateFeignApi.releaseStock(req);

                    log.info("超时释放成功: recordNo={}", record.getRecordNo());

                } catch (Exception e) {
                    log.error("超时释放失败: recordNo={}, error={}", record.getRecordNo(), e.getMessage());
                }
            }
        } finally {
            releaseLock(lockKey);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 2. 到期提醒：每天早 9 点扫描
     * BORROWED 且 remind_time 为空 且 due_date 在 3 天内
     */
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 0 9 * * ?")
    public void remindDueRecords() {
        String lockKey = LOCK_PREFIX + "remindDue";
        if (!tryLock(lockKey, 3600)) return;

        try {
            log.info("开始扫描到期提醒");
            LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
            LocalDateTime threeDaysLater = now.plusDays(3);

            List<BorrowRecord> list = borrowRecordMapper.selectList(
                    new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                            .eq(BorrowRecord::getStatus, "BORROWED")
                            .isNull(BorrowRecord::getRemindTime)
                            .lt(BorrowRecord::getDueDate, threeDaysLater)
                            .ge(BorrowRecord::getDueDate, now)
                            .last("LIMIT 100")
            );

            for (BorrowRecord record : list) {
                try {
                    // TODO: 发送通知（站内信/短信），MVP 先打日志
                    log.info("到期提醒: userId={}, recordNo={}, dueDate={}",
                            record.getUserId(), record.getRecordNo(), record.getDueDate());

                    // 标记已提醒
                    borrowRecordMapper.update(null,
                            new UpdateWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                                    .eq("id", record.getId())
                                    .set("remind_time", now));

                } catch (Exception e) {
                    log.error("提醒失败: recordNo={}", record.getRecordNo());
                }
            }
        } finally {
            releaseLock(lockKey);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 3. 逾期扫描：每天早 9 点 05 分
     * BORROWED 且 due_date < NOW() → 改为 OVERDUE
     */
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 5 9 * * ?")
    public void markOverdueRecords() {
        String lockKey = LOCK_PREFIX + "markOverdue";
        if (!tryLock(lockKey, 3600)) return;

        try {
            log.info("开始扫描逾期记录");
            LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();

            List<BorrowRecord> list = borrowRecordMapper.selectList(
                    new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                            .eq(BorrowRecord::getStatus, "BORROWED")
                            .lt(BorrowRecord::getDueDate, now)
                            .last("LIMIT 100")
            );

            for (BorrowRecord record : list) {
                try {
                    borrowRecordMapper.update(null,
                            new UpdateWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                                    .eq("id", record.getId())
                                    .eq("status", "BORROWED")
                                    .set("status", "OVERDUE"));

                    // TODO: 调 user 服务加逾期次数
                    log.info("标记逾期: recordNo={}", record.getRecordNo());

                } catch (Exception e) {
                    log.error("标记逾期失败: recordNo={}", record.getRecordNo());
                }
            }
        } finally {
            releaseLock(lockKey);
        }
    }

    private boolean tryLock(String key, long seconds) {
        Boolean success = redisTemplate.opsForValue().setIfAbsent(key, "1", seconds, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        return Boolean.TRUE.equals(success);
    }

    private void releaseLock(String key) {
        redisTemplate.delete(key);
    }
}
关键总结
表格
设计	说明
申领	Feign 调 user 查状态 → 本地查重 → Feign 调 donate 扣库存 → 生成记录（7 天截止）
超时释放	定时任务每 30 分钟扫 pickup_deadline < NOW()，改 CANCELLED，调 donate 释放库存
到期提醒	每天 9 点扫 due_date 在 3 天内且未提醒的
逾期标记	每天 9:05 扫 due_date < NOW() 且 BORROWED 的，改 OVERDUE
分布式锁	Redis SETNX，防止多实例同时跑任务
MQ	borrow 服务完全不用 MQ，只有 donate 服务用 MQ 做异步入库
下一步：你需要在 donate 服务里实现 DonateStockController 的 deductStock 和 releaseStock 接口，否则 borrow 申领会报 "库存不足"。
那你帮我实现一下这个远程调用缺失的这个吧

好，这是 shelf-donate 服务里缺失的 库存扣减/释放/归还 三个 Feign 接口的完整实现。
一、Service 接口
java
package com.shelf.donate.service;

import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.BookInstanceDTO;

public interface IDonateStockService {

    BookInstanceDTO deductStock(String isbn);

    void releaseStock(Long instanceId, String isbn);

    void returnStock(Long instanceId, String isbn);
}
二、ServiceImpl（核心，带乐观锁 + FIFO 分配）
java
package com.shelf.donate.service.impl;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.update.UpdateWrapper;
import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.BookInstanceDTO;
import com.shelf.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.shelf.common.result.ErrorCode;
import com.shelf.donate.entity.BookInstance;
import com.shelf.donate.entity.BookSku;
import com.shelf.donate.mapper.BookInstanceMapper;
import com.shelf.donate.mapper.BookSkuMapper;
import com.shelf.donate.service.IDonateStockService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class DonateStockServiceImpl implements IDonateStockService {

    private final BookSkuMapper bookSkuMapper;
    private final BookInstanceMapper bookInstanceMapper;

    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public BookInstanceDTO deductStock(String isbn) {
        // 1. 查 SKU 库存
        BookSku sku = bookSkuMapper.selectOne(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<BookSku>().eq(BookSku::getIsbn, isbn));
        if (sku == null || sku.getAvailableStock() <= 0) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "库存不足");
        }

        // 2. 乐观锁扣减可用库存
        int updated = bookSkuMapper.update(null,
                new UpdateWrapper<BookSku>()
                        .eq("id", sku.getId())
                        .eq("version", sku.getVersion())
                        .setSql("available_stock = available_stock - 1")
                        .setSql("version = version + 1"));
        if (updated == 0) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.SYSTEM_ERROR, "库存扣减冲突，请重试");
        }

        // 3. FIFO 分配最早入库的实体书（加 FOR UPDATE 防止并发分配同一本）
        BookInstance instance = bookInstanceMapper.selectOne(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<BookInstance>()
                        .eq(BookInstance::getIsbn, isbn)
                        .eq(BookInstance::getStatus, "AVAILABLE")
                        .orderByAsc(BookInstance::getCreateTime)
                        .last("LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE"));

        if (instance == null) {
            // 极端情况：SKU 有库存但实体书没了，抛异常让事务回滚
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.SYSTEM_ERROR, "实体书分配失败，库存数据异常");
        }

        // 4. 改实体书状态为 RESERVED（已预留）
        instance.setStatus("RESERVED");
        bookInstanceMapper.updateById(instance);

        log.info("库存扣减成功: isbn={}, instanceCode={}", isbn, instance.getInstanceCode());

        // 5. 组装返回
        BookInstanceDTO dto = new BookInstanceDTO();
        dto.setInstanceId(instance.getId());
        dto.setInstanceCode(instance.getInstanceCode());
        dto.setIsbn(isbn);
        dto.setTitle(sku.getTitle());
        dto.setLocation(instance.getLocation());
        return dto;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public void releaseStock(Long instanceId, String isbn) {
        // 幂等：查当前状态
        BookInstance instance = bookInstanceMapper.selectById(instanceId);
        if (instance == null) {
            log.warn("释放库存失败，实体书不存在: instanceId={}", instanceId);
            return;
        }
        if (!"RESERVED".equals(instance.getStatus())) {
            log.warn("释放库存幂等跳过，状态已为: {}", instance.getStatus());
            return;
        }

        // 1. 实体书回置 AVAILABLE
        instance.setStatus("AVAILABLE");
        bookInstanceMapper.updateById(instance);

        // 2. SKU 可用库存 +1
        BookSku sku = bookSkuMapper.selectOne(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<BookSku>().eq(BookSku::getIsbn, isbn));
        if (sku != null) {
            bookSkuMapper.update(null,
                    new UpdateWrapper<BookSku>()
                            .eq("id", sku.getId())
                            .setSql("available_stock = available_stock + 1")
                            .setSql("version = version + 1"));
        }

        log.info("库存释放成功: instanceId={}, isbn={}", instanceId, isbn);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public void returnStock(Long instanceId, String isbn) {
        BookInstance instance = bookInstanceMapper.selectById(instanceId);
        if (instance == null) {
            log.warn("归还入库失败，实体书不存在: instanceId={}", instanceId);
            return;
        }

        // 归还时状态可能是 BORROWED 或 OVERDUE
        if (!"BORROWED".equals(instance.getStatus()) && !"OVERDUE".equals(instance.getStatus())) {
            log.warn("归还入库幂等跳过，状态已为: {}", instance.getStatus());
            return;
        }

        // 1. 改回 AVAILABLE
        instance.setStatus("AVAILABLE");
        bookInstanceMapper.updateById(instance);

        // 2. SKU 可用库存 +1
        BookSku sku = bookSkuMapper.selectOne(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<BookSku>().eq(BookSku::getIsbn, isbn));
        if (sku != null) {
            bookSkuMapper.update(null,
                    new UpdateWrapper<BookSku>()
                            .eq("id", sku.getId())
                            .setSql("available_stock = available_stock + 1")
                            .setSql("version = version + 1"));
        }

        log.info("归还入库成功: instanceId={}, isbn={}", instanceId, isbn);
    }
}
三、Controller（对外暴露给 borrow 服务 Feign 调用）
java
package com.shelf.donate.controller;

import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.*;
import com.shelf.common.result.Result;
import com.shelf.donate.service.IDonateStockService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/donate")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class DonateStockController {

    private final IDonateStockService donateStockService;

    /**
     * 申领扣库存（borrow 服务调用）
     */
    @PostMapping("/stock/deduct")
    public Result<BookInstanceDTO> deductStock(@RequestBody DeductStockRequest request) {
        BookInstanceDTO dto = donateStockService.deductStock(request.getIsbn());
        return Result.success(dto);
    }

    /**
     * 超时释放库存（borrow 定时任务调用）
     */
    @PostMapping("/stock/release")
    public Result<Void> releaseStock(@RequestBody ReleaseStockRequest request) {
        donateStockService.releaseStock(request.getInstanceId(), request.getIsbn());
        return Result.success();
    }

    /**
     * 归还入库（borrow 归还时调用）
     */
    @PostMapping("/stock/return")
    public Result<Void> returnStock(@RequestBody ReturnStockRequest request) {
        donateStockService.returnStock(request.getInstanceId(), request.getIsbn());
        return Result.success();
    }
}
四、关键设计说明
表格
接口	核心逻辑	防并发手段
deductStock	乐观锁扣 SKU 库存 → FIFO 分配实体书（FOR UPDATE）→ 改 RESERVED	version 乐观锁 + FOR UPDATE 行锁
releaseStock	查状态幂等 → 改 AVAILABLE → SKU 库存 +1	状态机幂等（只有 RESERVED 才执行）
returnStock	查状态幂等 → 改 AVAILABLE → SKU 库存 +1	状态机幂等（只有 BORROWED/OVERDUE 才执行）
为什么 deductStock 不用 Redis 了？
donate 服务内部操作，直接走 DB 乐观锁 + 行锁足够防超卖
MVP 阶段少一个组件少一个坑，面试时说"后续加 Redis 做前置库存校验"
FOR UPDATE 生效的前提：必须在 @Transactional 事务内，且 MySQL 隔离级别是 RR（可重复读） 或 RC（读已提交）。
五、现在 donate 服务的完整接口清单
plain
POST /api/donate              ← 捐赠提交（已有）
GET  /api/donate/my-records   ← 我的捐赠（已有）
GET  /api/donate/course/majors
GET  /api/donate/course/courses
GET  /api/donate/course/books

POST /api/donate/stock/deduct   ← 新增：申领扣库存
POST /api/donate/stock/release  ← 新增：释放库存
POST /api/donate/stock/return   ← 新增：归还入库
borrow 服务的 DonateFeignApi 已经定义了这 3 个接口，现在 donate 这边也实现了，两端对上了。
下一步你可以测试：
捐赠一本书（donate）→ 数据库有 AVAILABLE 状态的实体书
申领这本书（borrow）→ deductStock 扣减成功，返回 BookInstanceDTO
等 30 分钟或手动改 pickup_deadline 为过去时间 → 定时任务触发 releaseStock 释放库存

二、补全后的 UserFeignApi（新增逾期接口）
java
package com.shelf.api.user;

import com.shelf.api.user.dto.UserStatusDTO;
import com.shelf.common.result.Result;
import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.FeignClient;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;

@FeignClient(name = "shelf-user", path = "/api/user")
public interface UserFeignApi {

    @GetMapping("/{userId}/status")
    Result<UserStatusDTO> getUserStatus(@PathVariable("userId") Long userId);

    /**
     * 增加用户逾期次数（borrow 定时任务调用）
     */
    @PostMapping("/{userId}/overdue")
    Result<Void> increaseOverdue(@PathVariable("userId") Long userId);
}
三、UserController 里补上加逾期接口
在 shelf-user 模块的 UserController.java 里加上：
java
/**
 * 增加逾期次数（borrow 定时任务调用）
 */
@PostMapping("/{userId}/overdue")
public Result<Void> increaseOverdue(@PathVariable Long userId) {
    UserBorrowQuota quota = quotaService.getById(userId);
    if (quota != null) {
        quota.setOverdueCount(quota.getOverdueCount() + 1);
        quotaService.updateById(quota);
        log.info("用户逾期次数+1: userId={}, overdueCount={}", userId, quota.getOverdueCount());
    }
    return Result.success();
}

package com.shelf.api.donate;

import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.*;

import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.FeignClient;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;

@FeignClient(name = "shelf-donate", path = "/api/donate")
public interface DonateFeignApi {

    @PostMapping("/stock/deduct")
    Result<BookInstanceDTO> deductStock(@RequestBody DeductStockRequest request);

    @PostMapping("/stock/release")
    Result<Void> releaseStock(@RequestBody ReleaseStockRequest request);

    @PostMapping("/stock/return")
    Result<Void> returnStock(@RequestBody ReturnStockRequest request);
}

private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;这个是用来干什么的呀，是数据表的id吗，能替换成 @Serial
    private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;这样吗
编辑
复制
分享
UID作用与 @Serial 替换
serialVersionUID 不是数据表 ID，它是 Java 对象序列化时的版本号。
一、它是干嘛的？
你的 DTO 实现了 Serializable，会在 服务间通过网络传输（比如 Feign 调用时把对象转成字节流传过去）。
java
// 发送方（borrow 服务）把对象转成字节流
ObjectOutputStream.writeObject(dto);

// 接收方（donate 服务）把字节流转回对象
ObjectInputStream.readObject(dto);
serialVersionUID 就是告诉 JVM："这个类的版本是 1，如果接收方反序列化时发现版本号对不上，就抛异常，防止用旧版类结构去解析新版数据"。
如果你不写，JVM 会根据类名、字段、方法等自动算一个哈希值。一旦你改了 DTO 加了个字段，自动算出来的值就变了，旧消息就反序列化失败。
显式写死 1L 就是告诉 JVM："我自己管版本，类结构小改动也别报错，兼容处理。"
二、能替换成 @Serial 吗？
能，而且推荐。 你的项目是 Java 21，完全支持。
java
// 传统写法（兼容所有 Java 版本）
private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;

// Java 14+ 的简洁写法（你的 Java 21 完全支持）
@Serial
private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;
@Serial 是 Java 14 引入的标准注解，放在 java.io.Serial 包里。作用就是标记这个字段是序列化相关的，让编译器帮你检查：
是不是 static final long
名字是不是叫 serialVersionUID
写错会编译报错，更严谨。
三、实际建议
表格
写法	适用场景
private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;	最通用，导师/面试官都认识
@Serial private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;	Java 14+ 项目，更现代，代码更简洁
你的项目用哪个都行。 如果追求"技术感"和代码简洁，统一改成 @Serial 更好；如果担心导师问"这是啥注解"，保持传统写法也行。
功能没有任何区别。
好的，那我还缺失什么功能不，你再检查一下

复制
分享
优先实现捐赠与申领核心流程
我帮你从头到尾捋了一遍，核心链路里还缺 7 个关键东西。有些不补的话，Postman 都跑不通。
缺失清单（按优先级排）
表格
优先级	缺失项	不补的后果
🔴 P0	Gateway 路由配置	请求到不了 donate/borrow 服务，全部 404
🔴 P0	borrow_record 建表 SQL	borrow 服务启动报错，表不存在
🔴 P0	领取确认接口	申领后永远卡在 PENDING_PICKUP，变不成 BORROWED
🔴 P0	归还接口	书借出去永远回不来
🟡 P1	搜索教材接口	小红打开平台看不到书，无法申领
🟡 P1	申领幂等键（requestId）	用户狂点按钮会生成多条记录，借到多本
🟡 P1	申领/归还时更新 user 额度	user_borrow_quota.current_borrow_count 永远为 0，额度校验失效
🟢 P2	admin 扫码页面	MVP 可用 Postman 代替，但真实场景必须有人扫码确认
🟢 P2	预置 course_book 数据	下拉框没数据，捐不了书
🟢 P2	OSS 图片上传接口	前端可以先直传 OSS，后端只收 URL，暂不阻塞
现在补 P0 + P1 的代码

2. borrow_record 建表 SQL
sql
CREATE TABLE `borrow_record` (
    `id`              BIGINT UNSIGNED     NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT COMMENT '主键ID',
    `record_no`       VARCHAR(32)         NOT NULL COMMENT '凭证号，如 BR202608190001',
    `request_id`      VARCHAR(64)         NULL COMMENT '幂等请求ID',
    `user_id`         BIGINT UNSIGNED     NOT NULL COMMENT '申领人ID',
    `instance_id`     BIGINT UNSIGNED     NOT NULL COMMENT '分配的实体书ID',
    `isbn`            VARCHAR(16)         NOT NULL COMMENT 'ISBN冗余',
    `book_title`      VARCHAR(128)        NOT NULL DEFAULT '' COMMENT '书名冗余',
    `status`          VARCHAR(16)         NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING_PICKUP' COMMENT '状态',
    `borrow_time`     DATETIME            NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP COMMENT '申领时间',
    `pickup_deadline` DATETIME            NOT NULL COMMENT '领取截止时间（7天后）',
    `pickup_time`     DATETIME            NULL COMMENT '实际领取时间',
    `due_date`        DATETIME            NULL COMMENT '到期时间（一学期后）',
    `return_time`     DATETIME            NULL COMMENT '实际归还时间',
    `remind_time`     DATETIME            NULL COMMENT '提醒发送时间',
    `qr_code`         VARCHAR(256)        NOT NULL DEFAULT '' COMMENT '二维码内容',
    `create_time`     DATETIME            NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP COMMENT '创建时间',
    `update_time`     DATETIME            NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP COMMENT '更新时间',
    PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
    UNIQUE KEY `uk_record_no` (`record_no`) USING BTREE,
    UNIQUE KEY `uk_request_id` (`request_id`) USING BTREE,
    KEY `idx_user_status` (`user_id`, `status`) USING BTREE,
    KEY `idx_instance_status` (`instance_id`, `status`) USING BTREE,
    KEY `idx_status_pickup_deadline` (`status`, `pickup_deadline`) USING BTREE,
    KEY `idx_status_due_date` (`status`, `due_date`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='借阅记录';

DonateStockServiceImpl 加：
java
@Override
public List<BookSku> searchSku(String keyword, Integer grade, String major) {
    LambdaQueryWrapper<BookSku> wrapper = new LambdaQueryWrapper<>();
    if (StrUtil.isNotBlank(keyword)) {
        wrapper.and(w -> w.like(BookSku::getTitle, keyword)
                         .or()
                         .like(BookSku::getIsbn, keyword));
    }
    // 如果需要按年级专业过滤，需要连表 course_book，MVP 先简单搜索
    wrapper.orderByDesc(BookSku::getAvailableStock);
    return bookSkuMapper.selectList(wrapper);
}

BorrowServiceImpl 加：
java
@Override
@Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
public void pickupConfirm(String recordNo) {
    BorrowRecord record = baseMapper.selectOne(
            new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                    .eq(BorrowRecord::getRecordNo, recordNo));
    if (record == null) {
        throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "凭证不存在");
    }
    if (!"PENDING_PICKUP".equals(record.getStatus())) {
        throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "该记录已处理或状态异常");
    }

    // 1. 更新借阅记录
    record.setStatus("BORROWED");
    record.setPickupTime(LocalDateTime.now());
    record.setDueDate(LocalDateTime.now().plusMonths(4)); // 一学期 4 个月
    baseMapper.updateById(record);

    // 2. 更新用户额度（currentBorrowCount +1）
    userFeignApi.increaseBorrowCount(record.getUserId());

    log.info("领取确认成功: recordNo={}", recordNo);
}

@Override
@Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
public void returnBook(String recordNo) {
    BorrowRecord record = baseMapper.selectOne(
            new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                    .eq(BorrowRecord::getRecordNo, recordNo));
    if (record == null) {
        throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "凭证不存在");
    }
    if (!"BORROWED".equals(record.getStatus()) && !"OVERDUE".equals(record.getStatus())) {
        throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "该记录不可归还");
    }

    // 1. 调 donate 归还入库
    ReturnStockRequest req = new ReturnStockRequest();
    req.setInstanceId(record.getInstanceId());
    req.setIsbn(record.getIsbn());
    donateFeignApi.returnStock(req);

    // 2. 更新借阅记录
    record.setStatus("RETURNED");
    record.setReturnTime(LocalDateTime.now());
    baseMapper.updateById(record);

    // 3. 更新用户额度（currentBorrowCount -1）
    userFeignApi.decreaseBorrowCount(record.getUserId());

    log.info("归还成功: recordNo={}", recordNo);
}
5. 用户额度更新接口（user 服务）
UserFeignApi 加：
java
@PostMapping("/{userId}/borrow-count/increase")
Result<Void> increaseBorrowCount(@PathVariable("userId") Long userId);

@PostMapping("/{userId}/borrow-count/decrease")
Result<Void> decreaseBorrowCount(@PathVariable("userId") Long userId);
UserController 加：
java
@PostMapping("/{userId}/borrow-count/increase")
public Result<Void> increaseBorrowCount(@PathVariable Long userId) {
    UserBorrowQuota quota = quotaService.getById(userId);
    if (quota != null) {
        quota.setCurrentBorrowCount(quota.getCurrentBorrowCount() + 1);
        quota.setTotalBorrowCount(quota.getTotalBorrowCount() + 1);
        quotaService.updateById(quota);
    }
    return Result.success();
}

@PostMapping("/{userId}/borrow-count/decrease")
public Result<Void> decreaseBorrowCount(@PathVariable Long userId) {
    UserBorrowQuota quota = quotaService.getById(userId);
    if (quota != null && quota.getCurrentBorrowCount() > 0) {
        quota.setCurrentBorrowCount(quota.getCurrentBorrowCount() - 1);
        quotaService.updateById(quota);
    }
    return Result.success();
}

BorrowServiceImpl.apply() 里加校验：
java
// 在"检查是否已借过同一 ISBN"之后，"Feign 调 donate 扣库存"之前加：

// 幂等：同一 requestId 直接返回上次结果
if (StrUtil.isNotBlank(dto.getRequestId())) {
    BorrowRecord exist = baseMapper.selectOne(
            new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                    .eq(BorrowRecord::getRequestId, dto.getRequestId()));
    if (exist != null) {
        // 返回已存在的记录（前端超时重试时直接返回）
        BorrowRecordVO vo = new BorrowRecordVO();
        BeanUtil.copyProperties(exist, vo);
        // TODO: 需要回填 instanceCode/location，可以从 donate 查或冗余存储
        return vo;
    }
}

// 生成记录时加上 requestId
record.setRequestId(dto.getRequestId());

不需要替换，你这份 SQL 已经是完整的最终版。

二、shelf-api（Feign 契约补全）
com.shelf.api.user.UserFeignApi
java
package com.shelf.api.user;

import com.shelf.api.user.dto.UserStatusDTO;
import com.shelf.common.result.Result;
import org.springframework.cloud.openfeign.FeignClient;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;

@FeignClient(name = "shelf-user", path = "/api/user")
public interface UserFeignApi {

    @GetMapping("/{userId}/status")
    Result<UserStatusDTO> getUserStatus(@PathVariable("userId") Long userId);

    @PostMapping("/{userId}/borrow-count/increase")
    Result<Void> increaseBorrowCount(@PathVariable("userId") Long userId);

    @PostMapping("/{userId}/borrow-count/decrease")
    Result<Void> decreaseBorrowCount(@PathVariable("userId") Long userId);

    @PostMapping("/{userId}/overdue")
    Result<Void> increaseOverdue(@PathVariable("userId") Long userId);
}
三、shelf-user（额度更新接口）
com.shelf.user.controller.UserController（在原有基础上追加两个方法）
java
    /**
     * 增加用户当前借阅数（borrow 领取时调用）
     */
    @PostMapping("/{userId}/borrow-count/increase")
    public Result<Void> increaseBorrowCount(@PathVariable Long userId) {
        UserBorrowQuota quota = quotaService.getById(userId);
        if (quota != null) {
            quota.setCurrentBorrowCount(quota.getCurrentBorrowCount() + 1);
            quota.setTotalBorrowCount(quota.getTotalBorrowCount() + 1);
            quotaService.updateById(quota);
            log.info("用户借阅数+1: userId={}", userId);
        }
        return Result.success();
    }

    /**
     * 减少用户当前借阅数（borrow 归还时调用）
     */
    @PostMapping("/{userId}/borrow-count/decrease")
    public Result<Void> decreaseBorrowCount(@PathVariable Long userId) {
        UserBorrowQuota quota = quotaService.getById(userId);
        if (quota != null && quota.getCurrentBorrowCount() > 0) {
            quota.setCurrentBorrowCount(quota.getCurrentBorrowCount() - 1);
            quotaService.updateById(quota);
            log.info("用户借阅数-1: userId={}", userId);
        }
        return Result.success();
    }

            wrapper.and(w -> w.like(BookSku::getTitle, keyword)
                    .or()

2. Controller（完整替换）
com.shelf.borrow.controller.BorrowController
java
package com.shelf.borrow.controller;

import cn.dev33.satoken.annotation.SaCheckLogin;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.dto.ApplyDTO;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.dto.PickupConfirmDTO;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.dto.ReturnDTO;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.vo.BorrowRecordVO;
import com.shelf.borrow.service.IBorrowService;
import com.shelf.common.result.Result;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.util.List;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/borrow")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class BorrowController {

    private final IBorrowService borrowService;

    @PostMapping("/apply")
    @SaCheckLogin
    public Result<BorrowRecordVO> apply(@Valid @RequestBody ApplyDTO dto) {
        return Result.success(borrowService.apply(dto));
    }

    @PostMapping("/pickup")
    @SaCheckLogin
    public Result<Void> pickupConfirm(@Valid @RequestBody PickupConfirmDTO dto) {
        borrowService.pickupConfirm(dto.getRecordNo());
        return Result.success();
    }

    @PostMapping("/return")
    @SaCheckLogin
    public Result<Void> returnBook(@Valid @RequestBody ReturnDTO dto) {
        borrowService.returnBook(dto.getRecordNo());
        return Result.success();
    }

    @GetMapping("/my-records")
    @SaCheckLogin
    public Result<List<BorrowRecordVO>> listMyBorrows() {
        return Result.success(borrowService.listMyBorrows());
    }
}
3. Service 接口（完整替换）
com.shelf.borrow.service.IBorrowService
java
package com.shelf.borrow.service;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.service.IService;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.dto.ApplyDTO;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.vo.BorrowRecordVO;
import com.shelf.borrow.entity.BorrowRecord;

import java.util.List;

public interface IBorrowService extends IService<BorrowRecord> {

    BorrowRecordVO apply(ApplyDTO dto);

    void pickupConfirm(String recordNo);

    void returnBook(String recordNo);

    List<BorrowRecordVO> listMyBorrows();
}
4. ServiceImpl（完整替换）
com.shelf.borrow.service.impl.BorrowServiceImpl
java
package com.shelf.borrow.service.impl;

import cn.hutool.core.bean.BeanUtil;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.service.impl.ServiceImpl;
import com.shelf.api.donate.DonateFeignApi;
import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.BookInstanceDTO;
import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.DeductStockRequest;
import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.ReleaseStockRequest;
import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.ReturnStockRequest;
import com.shelf.api.user.UserFeignApi;
import com.shelf.api.user.dto.UserStatusDTO;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.dto.ApplyDTO;
import com.shelf.borrow.domain.vo.BorrowRecordVO;
import com.shelf.borrow.entity.BorrowRecord;
import com.shelf.borrow.mapper.BorrowRecordMapper;
import com.shelf.borrow.service.IBorrowService;
import com.shelf.common.context.UserContext;
import com.shelf.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.shelf.common.result.ErrorCode;
import com.shelf.common.result.Result;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class BorrowServiceImpl extends ServiceImpl<BorrowRecordMapper, BorrowRecord> implements IBorrowService {

    private final UserFeignApi userFeignApi;
    private final DonateFeignApi donateFeignApi;
    private final StringRedisTemplate redisTemplate;

    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public BorrowRecordVO apply(ApplyDTO dto) {
        Long userId = UserContext.getUserId();
        if (userId == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.UNAUTHORIZED, "用户未登录");
        }

        // 0. 幂等：同一 requestId 直接返回上次结果
        if (dto.getRequestId() != null && !dto.getRequestId().isBlank()) {
            BorrowRecord exist = baseMapper.selectOne(
                    new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                            .eq(BorrowRecord::getRequestId, dto.getRequestId()));
            if (exist != null) {
                log.info("申领幂等命中: requestId={}", dto.getRequestId());
                BorrowRecordVO vo = new BorrowRecordVO();
                BeanUtil.copyProperties(exist, vo);
                return vo;
            }
        }

        // 1. Feign 调 user 查状态
        Result<UserStatusDTO> userResult = userFeignApi.getUserStatus(userId);
        if (!userResult.isSuccess() || userResult.getData() == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.SYSTEM_ERROR, "用户服务调用失败");
        }
        UserStatusDTO userStatus = userResult.getData();
        if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(userStatus.getFrozen())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, "账号已冻结，无法申领");
        }
        if (userStatus.getCurrentBorrowCount() != null && userStatus.getCurrentBorrowCount() >= 5) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, "借阅数量已达上限（5本）");
        }

        // 2. 检查是否已借过同一 ISBN（未归还）
        Long existCount = baseMapper.selectCount(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                        .eq(BorrowRecord::getUserId, userId)
                        .eq(BorrowRecord::getIsbn, dto.getIsbn())
                        .in(BorrowRecord::getStatus, Arrays.asList("PENDING_PICKUP", "BORROWED", "OVERDUE"))
        );
        if (existCount != null && existCount > 0) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "您已借阅该教材，归还后可再次申领");
        }

        // 3. Feign 调 donate 扣库存
        DeductStockRequest deductReq = new DeductStockRequest();
        deductReq.setIsbn(dto.getIsbn());
        Result<BookInstanceDTO> deductResult = donateFeignApi.deductStock(deductReq);
        if (!deductResult.isSuccess() || deductResult.getData() == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "库存不足或扣减失败");
        }
        BookInstanceDTO instance = deductResult.getData();

        try {
            // 4. 生成本地借阅记录
            BorrowRecord record = new BorrowRecord();
            record.setRecordNo(generateRecordNo());
            record.setRequestId(dto.getRequestId());
            record.setUserId(userId);
            record.setInstanceId(instance.getInstanceId());
            record.setIsbn(dto.getIsbn());
            record.setBookTitle(instance.getTitle());
            record.setStatus("PENDING_PICKUP");
            record.setBorrowTime(LocalDateTime.now());
            record.setPickupDeadline(LocalDateTime.now().plusDays(7));
            record.setQrCode(generateQrCode(record.getRecordNo()));
            baseMapper.insert(record);

            // 5. 组装返回
            BorrowRecordVO vo = new BorrowRecordVO();
            BeanUtil.copyProperties(record, vo);
            vo.setInstanceCode(instance.getInstanceCode());
            vo.setLocation(instance.getLocation());
            return vo;

        } catch (Exception e) {
            // 补偿：释放库存
            log.error("申领失败，补偿释放库存: instanceId={}, isbn={}", instance.getInstanceId(), dto.getIsbn());
            ReleaseStockRequest releaseReq = new ReleaseStockRequest();
            releaseReq.setInstanceId(instance.getInstanceId());
            releaseReq.setIsbn(dto.getIsbn());
            try {
                donateFeignApi.releaseStock(releaseReq);
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                log.error("补偿释放库存失败: {}", ex.getMessage());
            }
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.SYSTEM_ERROR, "申领失败，请重试");
        }
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public void pickupConfirm(String recordNo) {
        BorrowRecord record = baseMapper.selectOne(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                        .eq(BorrowRecord::getRecordNo, recordNo));
        if (record == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "凭证不存在");
        }
        if (!"PENDING_PICKUP".equals(record.getStatus())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "该记录已处理或状态异常，当前状态：" + record.getStatus());
        }

        // 更新为已借出
        record.setStatus("BORROWED");
        record.setPickupTime(LocalDateTime.now());
        record.setDueDate(LocalDateTime.now().plusMonths(4)); // 一学期4个月
        baseMapper.updateById(record);

        // 更新用户额度
        userFeignApi.increaseBorrowCount(record.getUserId());

        log.info("领取确认成功: recordNo={}, userId={}", recordNo, record.getUserId());
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(rollbackFor = Exception.class)
    public void returnBook(String recordNo) {
        BorrowRecord record = baseMapper.selectOne(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                        .eq(BorrowRecord::getRecordNo, recordNo));
        if (record == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "凭证不存在");
        }
        if (!"BORROWED".equals(record.getStatus()) && !"OVERDUE".equals(record.getStatus())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.PARAM_ERROR, "该记录不可归还，当前状态：" + record.getStatus());
        }

        // 1. 调 donate 归还入库
        ReturnStockRequest req = new ReturnStockRequest();
        req.setInstanceId(record.getInstanceId());
        req.setIsbn(record.getIsbn());
        donateFeignApi.returnStock(req);

        // 2. 更新记录
        record.setStatus("RETURNED");
        record.setReturnTime(LocalDateTime.now());
        baseMapper.updateById(record);

        // 3. 更新用户额度
        userFeignApi.decreaseBorrowCount(record.getUserId());

        log.info("归还成功: recordNo={}, userId={}", recordNo, record.getUserId());
    }

    @Override
    public List<BorrowRecordVO> listMyBorrows() {
        Long userId = UserContext.getUserId();
        List<BorrowRecord> list = baseMapper.selectList(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                        .eq(BorrowRecord::getUserId, userId)
                        .orderByDesc(BorrowRecord::getCreateTime));
        return list.stream().map(r -> {
            BorrowRecordVO vo = new BorrowRecordVO();
            BeanUtil.copyProperties(r, vo);
            return vo;
        }).collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    private String generateRecordNo() {
        String day = LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMMdd"));
        String key = "shelf:borrow:seq:" + day;
        Long seq = redisTemplate.opsForValue().increment(key);
        if (seq == null) seq = 1L;
        return String.format("BR%s%06d", day, seq);
    }

    private String generateQrCode(String recordNo) {
        return java.util.Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(recordNo.getBytes());
    }
}
5. 定时任务（完整替换，补上逾期调用）
com.shelf.borrow.task.BorrowScheduledTask
java
package com.shelf.borrow.task;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.update.UpdateWrapper;
import com.shelf.api.donate.DonateFeignApi;
import com.shelf.api.donate.dto.ReleaseStockRequest;
import com.shelf.api.user.UserFeignApi;
import com.shelf.borrow.entity.BorrowRecord;
import com.shelf.borrow.mapper.BorrowRecordMapper;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

@Slf4j
@Component
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class BorrowScheduledTask {

    private final BorrowRecordMapper borrowRecordMapper;
    private final DonateFeignApi donateFeignApi;
    private final UserFeignApi userFeignApi;  // ← 新增注入
    private final StringRedisTemplate redisTemplate;

    private static final String LOCK_PREFIX = "shelf:task:lock:";

    /**
     * 1. 超时释放：每 30 分钟扫描一次
     */
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 0/30 * * * ?")
    public void releaseTimeoutRecords() {
        String lockKey = LOCK_PREFIX + "releaseTimeout";
        if (!tryLock(lockKey, 600)) {
            log.info("超时释放任务已被其他实例执行，跳过");
            return;
        }

        try {
            log.info("开始扫描超时未领取记录");
            LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();

            List<BorrowRecord> timeoutList = borrowRecordMapper.selectList(
                    new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                            .eq(BorrowRecord::getStatus, "PENDING_PICKUP")
                            .lt(BorrowRecord::getPickupDeadline, now)
                            .last("LIMIT 100")
            );

            for (BorrowRecord record : timeoutList) {
                try {
                    borrowRecordMapper.update(null,
                            new UpdateWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                                    .eq("id", record.getId())
                                    .eq("status", "PENDING_PICKUP")
                                    .set("status", "CANCELLED"));

                    ReleaseStockRequest req = new ReleaseStockRequest();
                    req.setInstanceId(record.getInstanceId());
                    req.setIsbn(record.getIsbn());
                    donateFeignApi.releaseStock(req);

                    log.info("超时释放成功: recordNo={}", record.getRecordNo());

                } catch (Exception e) {
                    log.error("超时释放失败: recordNo={}, error={}", record.getRecordNo(), e.getMessage());
                }
            }
        } finally {
            releaseLock(lockKey);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 2. 到期提醒：每天早 9 点扫描
     */
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 0 9 * * ?")
    public void remindDueRecords() {
        String lockKey = LOCK_PREFIX + "remindDue";
        if (!tryLock(lockKey, 3600)) return;

        try {
            log.info("开始扫描到期提醒");
            LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
            LocalDateTime threeDaysLater = now.plusDays(3);

            List<BorrowRecord> list = borrowRecordMapper.selectList(
                    new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                            .eq(BorrowRecord::getStatus, "BORROWED")
                            .isNull(BorrowRecord::getRemindTime)
                            .lt(BorrowRecord::getDueDate, threeDaysLater)
                            .ge(BorrowRecord::getDueDate, now)
                            .last("LIMIT 100")
            );

            for (BorrowRecord record : list) {
                try {
                    log.info("到期提醒: userId={}, recordNo={}, dueDate={}",
                            record.getUserId(), record.getRecordNo(), record.getDueDate());

                    borrowRecordMapper.update(null,
                            new UpdateWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                                    .eq("id", record.getId())
                                    .set("remind_time", now));

                } catch (Exception e) {
                    log.error("提醒失败: recordNo={}", record.getRecordNo());
                }
            }
        } finally {
            releaseLock(lockKey);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 3. 逾期扫描：每天早 9 点 05 分
     */
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 5 9 * * ?")
    public void markOverdueRecords() {
        String lockKey = LOCK_PREFIX + "markOverdue";
        if (!tryLock(lockKey, 3600)) return;

        try {
            log.info("开始扫描逾期记录");
            LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();

            List<BorrowRecord> list = borrowRecordMapper.selectList(
                    new LambdaQueryWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                            .eq(BorrowRecord::getStatus, "BORROWED")
                            .lt(BorrowRecord::getDueDate, now)
                            .last("LIMIT 100")
            );

            for (BorrowRecord record : list) {
                try {
                    borrowRecordMapper.update(null,
                            new UpdateWrapper<BorrowRecord>()
                                    .eq("id", record.getId())
                                    .eq("status", "BORROWED")
                                    .set("status", "OVERDUE"));

                    // 增加用户逾期次数
                    userFeignApi.increaseOverdue(record.getUserId());

                    log.info("标记逾期: recordNo={}", record.getRecordNo());

                } catch (Exception e) {
                    log.error("标记逾期失败: recordNo={}", record.getRecordNo());
                }
            }
        } finally {
            releaseLock(lockKey);
        }
    }

    private boolean tryLock(String key, long seconds) {
        Boolean success = redisTemplate.opsForValue().setIfAbsent(key, "1", seconds, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        return Boolean.TRUE.equals(success);
    }

    private void releaseLock(String key) {
        redisTemplate.delete(key);
    }
}
现在能跑通的完整链路
plain
注册/登录 → 捐赠（MQ异步入库）→ 搜索教材 → 申领（幂等+Feign扣库存）
  → 领取确认（额度+1）→ 归还（Feign回库+额度-1）
  → 定时任务：超时释放 / 到期提醒 / 逾期标记
还缺但可后期补的：
前端页面（用 Postman 先测）
OSS 图片上传（前端直传，后端只收 URL）
预置 course_book 数据（手动 INSERT 几条测试数据即可）
下一步建议：先启动 Nacos/MySQL/Redis/RabbitMQ，按顺序启动 user → donate → borrow → gateway，用 Postman 跑一遍捐赠→申领→领取→归还的全流程。有报错随时贴给我。
好了，下面一步我可以干啥呀，除了测试之外，就是功能这边的还可以有什么呀，完整了吗，管理这边的呢
